•﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿ First Law (elliptical orbits): Every seat's orbit is an ellipse with the artifact at one focus. No seat
m﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿aintains constant analytical distance -- every seat alternates between closer (deeper) and farther

•﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍﻿ Second Law (equal areas in equal times): A seat sweeps equal analytical area in equal time. When
c﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍﻿lose to the artifact (periapsis), it moves fast through dense material -- deep, focused findings. When far

S﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿eats are organized into four shells, each with distinct orbital parameters and distinct audit functions. The shell

•﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍﻿ Period: 1 (audits every version)

3﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿.2 The fractal dimension of gate coverage
L﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿et N_total = total number of verifiable properties at a given level.
L﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿et N_gated = number of those properties checked by a gate.
F﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿ractal dimension of coverage: D = log(N_gated) / log(N_total)
I﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿f D approaches 0: the gates are a sparse dust (Cantor set) -- mostly ungated.
I﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍﻿f D approaches 1: the gates form a connected curve -- linear coverage.
I﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿f D approaches 2: the gates cover a surface -- dense coverage.
E﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿mpirically for the XZ Protocol at v0.47:
•﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍﻿ N_total ~ 200+ verifiable properties per entry (counts, IDs, claims, states)
•﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿ N_gated ~ 15 (the [9c/d/e/f], [2/2c/2d], [3/3a], [5] gates)
•﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿ D ~ log(15) / log(200) ~ 0.51

3﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿.3 The coverage improvement trajectory
E﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌‌‌‍﻿ach round adds gates: [2d] self-version at v0.47, [9c] check() potentially at v0.48. The fractal dimension
i﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍﻿ncreases, but slowly. The question is whether D can approach 1.0 (connected coverage) before the analytical
s﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍﻿urface grows faster than the gate set.

4﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿. The correction methodology: operating at the recursion rule
4﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿.1 The Level 0 intervention
T﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿he fractal cube model prescribes a specific correction methodology for self-similar defect classes:
D﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​​‌‍﻿o not patch Level N outputs. Modify the Level 0 recursion rule.
F﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿or the Axiom-3 narration class, the Level 0 recursion rule is:
C﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿URRENT RULE (memory-driven):
1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿. Read the audit findings
2﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿. Recall what the findings said

B﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿ayyinahEnterprise -- Unified Audit Methodology v0.1 -- Page 29

﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿3. Write prose describing the findings from recall
4﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿. Compute values from recall
5﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿. Seal the entry
C﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍﻿ORRECTED RULE (byte-driven):
1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿. Read the audit findings
2﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿. For each claim to be written:
a﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍﻿. Formulate the claim as a grep/sha256sum/python query
b﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍﻿. Execute the query against the shipped bytes
c﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿. Write the query output, not the recall
3﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍﻿. For each count to be stated:
a﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍﻿. Count from the bytes (grep -c, wc, python)
b﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿. Write the computed count, not the recalled count
4﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍﻿. For each finding ID to be cited:
a﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍﻿. Verify the ID exists in the source report bytes
b﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍﻿. Write the verified ID, not the recalled ID
5﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿. Run build.py (checks edge properties)
6﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿. Run a NEW face-interior verifier (checks prose claims against bytes)
7﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍﻿. Seal only if both pass

4﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿.2 The face-interior verifier
T﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿he fractal cube model identifies the structural gap: build.py checks edges but not face interiors. The correction
m﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿ethodology requires a face-interior verifier -- a tool that checks the prose claims in the current entry against
t﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿he bytes they describe.
S﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍﻿pecification for a face-interior verifier (build.py Section [10]):
d﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍﻿ef verify_current_entry_claims(protocol_text, entry):
"﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍﻿""Check prose O-CLAIMs in the current entry against bytes."""
#﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿ [10a] Count verification
#﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿ Extract every (count, noun) pair from the entry
#﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿ Verify each count against bytes:
#﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿ "3 corrections" -> count correction markers in entry
#﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿ "10 items" -> count register items
#﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍﻿ "29 boundaries" -> count boundary list items

c﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿hecked mechanically. The fractal cube model says: these 19 are the retained cells at Level 1. The removed
c﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿ells -- the ungated prose, the face interiors -- are where verification depends on the auditor, not the structure.
